```python
db.execute("""
```
```python
SELECT AVG((julianday('now') - julianday(P.birthday)) / 365.25) AS avg_age
FROM Player AS P
INNER JOIN Player_Attributes AS PA
  ON P.player_api_id = PA.player_api_id
WHERE PA.sprint_speed >= 97
  AND CAST(strftime('%Y', PA.date) AS INTEGER) >= 2013
  AND CAST(strftime('%Y', PA.date) AS INTEGER) <= 2015
```
```python
""")
```

```python
db.execute("""
WITH yearly AS (
  SELECT c.Segment, c.CustomerID, SUM(y.Consumption) AS total_consumption
  FROM customers c
  JOIN yearmonth y ON c.CustomerID = y.CustomerID
  WHERE c.Currency = 'CZK'
    AND y.Date BETWEEN '201301' AND '201312'
  GROUP BY c.Segment, c.CustomerID
),
min_consumption AS (
  SELECT Segment, MIN(total_consumption) AS min_total_consumption
  FROM yearly
  GROUP BY Segment
),
annual_avg AS (
  SELECT y.Segment, AVG(y.total_consumption) AS annual_avg_consumption
  FROM yearly y
  JOIN min_consumption m
    ON y.Segment = m.Segment AND y.total_consumption = m.min_total_consumption
  GROUP BY y.Segment
)
SELECT
  (SELECT annual_avg_consumption FROM annual_avg WHERE Segment='SME')
  - (SELECT annual_avg_consumption FROM annual_avg WHERE Segment='LAM') AS SME_LAM,
  (SELECT annual_avg_consumption FROM annual_avg WHERE Segment='LAM')
  - (SELECT annual_avg_consumption FROM annual_avg WHERE Segment='KAM') AS LAM_KAM,
  (SELECT annual_avg_consumption FROM annual_avg WHERE Segment='KAM')
  - (SELECT annual_avg_consumption FROM annual_avg WHERE Segment='SME') AS KAM_SME;
""")
```

```python
db.execute("""
SELECT AVG(oxygen_count) AS avg_oxygen_atoms
FROM (
  SELECT a.molecule_id, SUM(a.element = 'o') AS oxygen_count
  FROM atom AS a
  WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM bond AS b
    WHERE b.molecule_id = a.molecule_id
      AND b.bond_type = '-'
  )
  GROUP BY a.molecule_id
) AS t;
""")
```

```python
SELECT DISTINCT s.School
FROM schools AS s
INNER JOIN frpm AS f ON f.CDSCode = s.CDSCode
INNER JOIN satscores AS sc ON sc.cds = s.CDSCode
WHERE f.`Free Meal Count (K-12)` / f.`Enrollment (K-12)` > 0.1
  AND sc.NumGE1500 > 0
ORDER BY s.School
""")
```
